Algorithm A (Projected Subgradient / Frank-Wolfe):
- h feasible.
- Compute vals and Cmax, active indices where C_l ≥ Cmax - tol (tol maybe 1e-4 or dynamic).

- Use backtracking on exact Cmax: try step, if C(h_new) < C(h) accept; else reduce step. Maybe add small random noise.
- Because exact Cmax is Lipschitz; step size decreases. This may converge slowly. Add momentum: v = μ v + g; h = proj_K(h - lr * v). But nonconvex.

Vectorized gradient loop:
```
def vals_weights_grad(x, beta):
    n=len(x)
    dx=2.0/n
    s0 = x.sum() - (x*x).sum()
    C0=dx*s0
    prefix=np.cumsum(x)
    total=prefix[-1]
    prod=np.correlate(x,x,'valid')  # length n-1
    Cplus=np.empty(n-1); Cminus=np.empty(n-1)
    for s in range(1,n):
        p_s = prefix[n-s] - prod[s-1]
        m_s = total - prefix[s] - prod[s-1]
        Cplus[s-1]=dx*p_s; Cminus[s-1]=dx*m_s
    C=np.empty(2*n-1); C[0]=C0; C[1:n]=Cplus; C[n:]=Cminus
    # weights
    cmax=C.max()
    # To avoid extreme, shift
    z=beta*(C-cmax)
    # if beta small? Use expz = np.exp(np.clip(z, -50, 50))
    w=expz/expz.sum()
    # H = cmax + np.log(expz.sum())/beta
    grad = np.empty(n)
    # s0
    wp=w[1:n]; wm=w[n:2*n-1]
    grad[:] = (w[0]*(1.0 - 2.0*x)) # Actually w0 weight; grad of C0
    for s in range(1,n):
        a=wp[s-1]; b=wm[s-1]
        if a == 0 and b == 0: continue
        # slice lengths
        left = 0; right=0? Use views
        h_left_x = x[:n-s]
        h_right = x[s:]
        if a:
            grad[:n-s] += a*(1.0 - h_right)
            grad[s:] -= a*h_left_x
        if b:
            grad[:n-s] -= b*h_right
            grad[s:] += b*(1.0 - h_left_x)
    return H, grad, cmax
```
Wait s0 weight: The gradient of C0 = dx*(1 - 2x) per element. In weighted sum ∑w_l C_l, s0 contribution w0 * dx*(1-2x). Our code should include dx. We omitted dx. Need include dx factor: grad_s0 = w0*dx*(1-2x); for s>0 gradient of C_s includes dx. In pair updates multiply by a*dx? We set weights w for C values in objective units (already dx). Gradient of C_s = dx*∇F_s. In the slice updates above, terms like (1-h_right) are ∇F; need multiply by dx. So either set a = w[s]*dx. We'll do `scale_dx=dx`, `a=wp[s-1]*dx`. Or include dx at end: compute G_f without dx then grad=dx*G. Since H uses C=dx F, gradient=dx ∇F. Let's compute Fvals and weights based on C but gradient based on F then multiply dx. Easier: `a=wp[s-1]*dx`, `b=wm[s-1]*dx`, `grad = w0*dx*(1-2x)`. Good.

```
_current_deadline = 0.0
_current_beta = 10.0
def _objective(x):
    if time.time() > _current_deadline: raise TimeLimit()
    H, g, _ = smooth_calc(x, _current_beta)
    return H
def _gradient(x):
    if time.time() > _current_deadline: raise TimeLimit()
    H,g,c = smooth_calc(x, _current_beta)
    return g
```

Implement `project_sequence(x, n)` robust:
```
def project_sequence(x,n):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).copy()
    # if n mismatches, truncate/pad?
    target=n/2.0
    lb=0.0; ub=1.0
    # find lambda in [ -1 - max(x)?, 1 - min(x)? ]
    lo=- (np.max(x) + 1e-15)
    hi=1.0 - np.min(x)
    # f(lambda)=sum(clip(x+lambda))-target
    # f(lo) <=0, f(hi)>=0 usually
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(x+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    p=np.clip(x+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # adjust residual:
    residual=target-p.sum()
    if abs(residual)>1e-12:
       # add to element closest to 0.5 with room
       idxs=np.where((p>1e-9)&(p<1-1e-9))[0]
       if len(idxs):
          idx=idxs[(idxs + np.arange(len(idxs))?)? choose max abs(p-0.5)?]
          room = min(max(residual,0), 1-p[idx]) if residual>0 else max(residual, p[idx]-1)
           p[idx]+=room
       else:
          # distribute tiny? If residual small, adjust first not bound
    return p
```
Bisection 60 iterations maybe overkill; 50 enough. Each project O(n). Many times okay. Could optimize with sorting? Bisection fine.
Need exact target. Floating sum may not exact; residual adjustment. If residual large due no room (should not), distribute over all: p += residual/n then clip? But target average .5, always feasible.

```
def exact_c5(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    n=len(h)
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)
```

Optimization of gradient loops:
```
for s in range(1,n):
    a = wp[s-1]*dx
    b = wm[s-1]*dx
    if a == 0.0 and b == 0.0: continue
    ls = n - s
    xleft = x[:ls]
    xright = x[s:]
    if a:
       grad[:ls] += a * (1.0 - xright)
       grad[s:] -= a * xleft
    if b:
       grad[:ls] -= b * xright
       grad[s:] += b * (1.0 - xleft)
```

Let's code `smooth_calc` robust:
```
def smooth_calc(x, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = x.size
    dx = 2.0/n
    # raw F values (unscaled) to avoid repeated dx? We can compute C directly.
    s0 = x.sum() - np.dot(x,x)
    C0 = dx*s0
    prefix = np.empty(n+1); prefix[0]=0; np.cumsum(x, out=prefix[1:])
    total=prefix[n]
    if n>1:
       prod = np.correlate(x,x,'valid')
       # If np.correlate of length1? n>1 ok.
       idx = np.arange(1,n)
       Cplus = dx*(prefix[n-idx] - prod)
       Cminus = dx*(total - prefix[idx] - prod)
    else:
       Cplus=Cminus=np.empty(0)
    C = np.concatenate(([C0], Cplus, Cminus))
    cmax = float(C.max())
    # If beta == 0? not, but if beta<=0 use uniform? maybe.
    if beta <= 1e-6:
       w = np.full_like(C, 1.0/C.size)
       H = cmax  #?
    else:
       z = beta*(C - cmax)
       # clip z to [-50, 50] (max 0, min -50)
       expz = np.exp(np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0))
       s = expz.sum()
       if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
           w = np.zeros_like(C); idx=np.argmax(C); w[idx]=1.0; H=cmax
       else:
           w = expz/s
           H = cmax + np.log(s)/beta
    # gradient weights scale by dx
    grad = np.empty(n)
    if n==1:
       # special? n not 1.
    w0 = w[0]*dx
    grad[:] = w0*(1.0 - 2.0*x)
    if n>1:
       wp = w[1:n]*dx
       wm = w[n:2*n-1]*dx
       # threshold
       # use for loop
       for s in range(1,n):
          a=wp[s-1]; b=wm[s-1]
          if (a < 1e-12 and b < 1e-12): continue
          ls = n - s
          # use views
          if a:
             grad[:ls] += a * (1.0 - x[s:])
             grad[s:] -= a * x[:ls]
          if b:
             grad[:ls] -= b * x[s:]
             grad[s:] += b * (1.0 - x[:ls])
    return H, grad, cmax
```
Check negative gradient pair formula: For Cminus pair (i=left, j=right): left derivative -h_j; right derivative 1-h_i. Code: `grad[:ls] -= b*x[s:]`; `grad[s:] += b*(1-x[:ls])`. Good.
Positive: left += a*(1-h_j); right -= a*h_i. Good.

Potential issue: `w[1:n]` length n-1; index for plus s=1 => w[1]. `w[n:2*n-1]` length n-1; s=1 => w[n]. Good.

`Cplus = dx*(prefix[n-idx] - prod)` where idx 1..n-1: prefix[n-s] sum h[0:n-s]. Good. `Cminus = dx*(total - prefix[s] - prod)` sum h[s:n]. Good.

`project_sequence` implement efficiently:
```
def project_sequence(x, n=None):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).copy()
    if n is None: n=x.size
    target=0.5*n
    # If size mismatch, adjust
    if x.size != n:
       # truncate/pad with 0.5
       y=np.full(n,0.5)
       m=min(n,x.size); y[:m]=x[:m]; x=y
    # Prelim normalize? Bisection on x as is.
    lo = - (float(x.max()) + 1e-12)
    hi = 1.0 - float(x.min())
    # Ensure f(lo)<=0? If x all 1, lo=-1 => sum0 <=target for n>0; hi=0 => sum n >=target. good.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
       s = np.clip(x+mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    y=np.clip(x+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    # fine adjust
    for _ in range(5):
       res = target - y.sum()
       if abs(res) < 1e-13: break
       exact_indices = np.where((y>1e-8)&(y<1-1e-8))[0]
       if exact_indices.size ==0:
           # add/distribute to all with largest room
           if res>0:
              rooms=1-y
           else:
              rooms=y
           idx = int(np.argmax(rooms))
       else:
           # choose index closest to 0.5 maybe
           idx = int(exact_indices[np.argmin(np.abs(y[exact_indices]-0.5))])
       if res >0:
           add = min(res, max(0.0, 1.0-y[idx]))
       else:
           add = max(res, min(0.0, y[idx]-1.0)) # negative
       y[idx] += add
    return y
```
The fine adjust loop with index closest to 0.5 may not bring residual to 0 if add limited; but small. Could distribute residual over all non-bound if not. Better:
```
if abs(res)>1e-12:
   mask=(y>1e-9)&(y<1-1e-9)
   if mask.any():
      y[mask] += res/mask.sum()
      y=np.clip(y,0,1)
   # if still residual, add to max room
```
This small change. Re-sum maybe. Since residual after bisection ~1e-14, fine.

```
def optimize_slsqp(h, n, beta, maxiter, deadline):
    global _current_deadline, _current_beta, _constraint_target
    x0 = project_sequence(h,n)
    _current_deadline = deadline
    _current_beta = beta
    _constraint_target = 0.5*n
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)]*n
    constraints = [{'type':'eq','fun':_eq_fun,'jac':_eq_jac}]
    options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-8,'disp':0}
    try:
       res=minimize(_obj_eq, x0, jac=_grad_eq, bounds=bounds, constraints=constraints, method='SLSQP', options=options)
       if res is not None and res.x is not None:
          x=res.x
       else: x=x0
    except TimeLimit:
       x=x0
    except Exception:
       x=x0
    x=project_sequence(x,n)
    c=exact_c5(x)
    return x,c
```
`_obj_eq` and `_grad_eq` top-level:
```
def _obj_eq(x):
   if time.time()>_current_deadline: raise TimeLimit()
   H,_,_ = smooth_calc(x, _current_beta)
   return float(H)
```
`_grad_eq` similar.

But `smooth_calc` called separately; time check. Good.

```
def optimize_lbgfs(h,n,beta,mu,maxiter,deadline):
   global _current_deadline,_current_beta,_pen_mu,_constraint_target
   x0=project_sequence(h,n)
   _pen_mu=mu
   ...
   res=minimize(_obj_pen,x0,jac=_grad_pen,bounds=bounds,method='L-BFGS-B',options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-8,'maxls':20,'disp':0})
   x=res.x; project; c; return
```

Now, possible mismatch of `np.correlate` sign in gradient: We assume set values. Even if exact C uses full correlate and our smooth_calc C differs by some shifts? We argued same set. But let's be absolutely sure. Numpy correlate a and v full: c[k] = ∑_m a[m] v[m - k + (len(v)-1)]? For equal lengths, k=-(n-1) to n-1. For k positive (a shifted right?), c[k] = ∑_m a[m+k] v[m] for m where valid. That gives ∑_{i=0}^{n-k-1} a[i+k] (1-a[i]) if k>0, which is our Cminus for shift k (right value times (1-left)). For k<0, c[k] = ∑ a[i] (1-a[i+|k|]) = our Cplus. So set same, order reversed. Good.

Now, exact C with correlate max same. Great.

Potential issue in `np.correlate(x,x,'valid')`: For x length n, valid output length n-1 but convention? It returns c[k] = ∑ x[i+k] x[i] for k=0..n-2, which is product sum for shift k. Symmetric, okay.

```
def make_sine_start(n, amp, phase):
    x = 2.0*np.arange(n)/n
    h = 0.5 + 0.5*amp*np.cos(np.pi*x - phase)
    return project_sequence(h,n)
```

`make_fourier_start`:
```
def make_fourier_start(n, seed, terms=6, max_freq=7, sym=False):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=2.0*np.arange(n)/n
    g=np.zeros(n)
    freqs = rng.choice(np.arange(1,max_freq+1), size=min(terms,max_freq), replace=False)
    for k in freqs:
       amp = 1.0/(k**0.7)
       if rng.random()<0.5: amp=-amp
       phase = 2*np.pi*rng.random()
       g += amp*np.cos(np.pi*k*x + phase)
    m=np.max(np.abs(g))
    if m>0: g/=m
    h=0.5+0.5*g
    # Clip? h already in [0,1] if g in [-1,1] yes
    return project_sequence(h,n)
```
Maybe include mean-zero? Cos terms mean over [0,2] not exactly zero at discrete if integer k? For integer k, ∑ cos(kπ i/n) over n? If n multiple? n maybe 45 odd, k integer, full periods? k period 2/k; over [0,2) discrete not exactly zero if n not multiple? Project fixes sum; but sum h may not 0.5; okay.

```
def make_blur_start(n, seed, sigma_frac=0.15):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    y=np.zeros(n)
    half=n//2
    if n%2==0: ys=rng.choice(n, half, replace=False); y[ys]=1
    else: ys=rng.choice(n, half, replace=False); y[ys]=1; y[rng.randint(n)]=0.5? But if duplicates? Could set y[ys[:-1]]=1 and one 0.5. Simpler: y = rng.rand(n); y[np.where(y>=0.5)]? But exact half not needed before project. Use random binary choose.
    sigma=max(2,int(n*sigma_frac))
    radius=5*sigma
    kernel = gaussian
    y = np.convolve(y,kernel,mode='same')
    return project_sequence(y,n)
```

```
def make_parent_upsample(parent,n,mode):

    m=len(parent)

       # if n multiple, tile? Use simple: y = np.empty(n); for i in range(n): y[i]=parent[int(i*m/n)]
```

Let's design `process_starts` in detail:
```
def process_starts(n, starts, deadline, max_start, optim_specs, seed):
    best_h=None; best_c=np.inf
    entries=[]
    count=0
    for st in starts:
        if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
        count +=1
        try:
            h = project_sequence(st, n)
            c1 = exact_c5(h)
            h2 = symmetrize(h)
            c2 = exact_c5(h2)
            if c2 < c1:
                h, c1 = h2, c2
            if c1 < best_c: best_h=h; best_c=c1
            entries.append((c1, count, h, c1)) # c1 duplicate
        except Exception:
            pass
    # sort entries by c then count
    entries.sort(key=None)  # tuple (c,count,h) default; h array may compare if c,count equal? count unique, so no.
    # selected: first min(max_start,len)
    for idx in range(min(max_start,len(entries))):
        if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
        _, _, h, c = entries[idx]
        # local best
        h_best=h; c_best=c
        for spec in optim_specs:
            if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
            kind = spec[0]; beta=spec[1]
            if kind == 'slsqp':
                maxiter = choose_maxiter_slsqp(n, deadline - time.time())
                h_new,c_new = optimize_slsqp(h_best,n,beta,maxiter,deadline-10)
            elif kind == 'lbgfs':
                maxiter = choose_maxiter_lbgfs(...)
                mu = spec[2] if len(spec)>2 else 50.0
                h_new,c_new = optimize_lbgfs(h_best,n,beta,mu,maxiter,deadline-10)
            else: continue
            if c_new < c_best:
                h_best=h_new; c_best=c_new
                # re-sym take better
                h_sym=symmetrize(h_best); c_sym=exact; if c_sym<c_best: h_best=h_sym; c_best=c_sym
        # random perturb once
        if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
        rng=np.random.default_rng(seed + idx*7919 + n)
        h_noisy = project_sequence(h_best + (rng.normal(0,0.02,size=n) - 0.0), n)
        h_noisy=sym? maybe eval both
        # optionally one lbgfs
        for spec in optim_specs[:1]:
           ...
        if c_best < best_c: best_h=h_best; best_c=c_best
    return best_h,best_c
```
Tuple entries: `(c1, count, h)` default sort compares h if c,count equal; count unique so no array comparison. Good.

```
if beta >= 12:
   thresh = max(1e-14, float(w.max())*1e-6)
   w = np.where(w > thresh, w, 0.0)
   sw=w.sum()
   if sw > 0: w = w/sw
   else: w=np.zeros_like(C); w[np.argmax(C)]=1.0
```

`_obj_eq` and `_grad_eq`:
```
def _obj_eq(x):
    if time.time() > _current_deadline:
        raise TimeLimitError()
    H, _, _ = smooth_calc(x, _current_beta)
    return float(H)
def _grad_eq(x):
    if time.time() > _current_deadline: raise TimeLimitError()
    _, g, _ = smooth_calc(x, _current_beta)
    return g
```

Optimize functions:
```
def optimize_slsqp(h,n,beta,maxiter,deadline):
    global _current_deadline,_current_beta,_constraint_target
    x0=project_sequence(h,n)
    # If maxiter <=0 return
    if maxiter <=0: return x0, exact_c5(x0)
    _current_deadline = max(deadline, time.time()+0.1)
    _current_beta = float(beta)
    _constraint_target = 0.5*float(n)
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type':'eq','fun':_eq_fun,'jac':_eq_jac}]
    options = {'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-8, 'disp': 0}
    try:
        res = minimize(_obj_eq, x0, jac=_grad_eq, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, method='SLSQP', options=options)
        if res is None: xbest=x0
        else:
            xbest = res.x if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None else x0
            # maybe res.x not feasible; project
    except TimeLimitError:
        xbest=x0
    except Exception:
        xbest=x0
    xbest = np.asarray(xbest, dtype=float).copy()
    xbest = project_sequence(xbest,n)
    c1=float(exact_c5(xbest))
    x2=symmetrize(xbest)
    c2=float(exact_c5(x2))
    if c2 < c1:
        xbest=x2; c1=c2
    return xbest,c1
```
Need if `res.x` close but not exactly; projection. If `optimize_slsqp` uses x0 as current if failure, returns c. Good.

```
def optimize_lbgfs(h,n,beta,mu,maxiter,deadline):

    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-8,'maxls':20,'disp':0}
    try: res=minimize(_obj_pen,x0,jac=_grad_pen,bounds=bounds,method='L-BFGS-B',options=options)
    ... project, sym, return
```

```
def gaussian_kernel(radius, sigma):
    t=np.arange(-radius,radius+1,dtype=float)
    k=np.exp(-0.5*(t/sigma)**2)
    k /= k.sum()
    return k
```
In blur, `radius = min(n, max(1, int(5*sigma)))`; kernel length. If kernel length > n? `np.convolve` same length; okay. But if radius large (n*1.0), kernel length 2n-1, convolution O(n*2n). `make_parent_upsample`:
```
def make_parent_upsample(parent,n,mode):
    if parent is None: return make_sine_start(n,1.0,0.0)
    m=len(parent)
    p=np.asarray(parent,dtype=float).ravel()
    if n==m: return project_sequence(p,n)
    if mode == 'repeat':
       idx=(np.arange(n)*m)//n
       y=p[idx]
    else:
       x_old=np.arange(m)*2.0/m
       x_new=np.arange(n)*2.0/n
       y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
    return project_sequence(y,n)
```
Endpoint=False. If mode invalid, interp.

`make_candidates`:
```
def make_candidates(n, seed, starter):
    starts=[]
    if starter is not None:
       starts.append(make_parent_upsample(starter,n,'repeat'))
       starts.append(make_parent_upsample(starter,n,'interp'))
       yi=make_parent_upsample(starter,n,'interp')
       starts.append(symmetrize(yi))
    # sine phases
    phases=[(1.0,0.0),(1.0,0.5),(1.0,-0.5),(0.9,0.0),(0.8,0.7)]
    for a,p in phases: starts.append(make_sine_start(n,a,p))
    if n <= 90:
       fourier_count=6; blur_counts={0.08:3,0.15:3,0.30:2}
    elif n <=200:
       fourier_count=4; blur_counts={0.10:2,0.20:2,0.35:1}
    else:
       fourier_count=2; blur_counts={0.12:1,0.25:1}
    for i in range(fourier_count): starts.append(make_fourier_start(n, seed+1000+n+i, 6, 7))
    for frac, cnt in blur_counts.items():
       for j in range(cnt): starts.append(make_blur_start(n, seed+2000+n+j+int(frac*100), frac))
    return starts
```
Sorting `blur_counts.items` dict order arbitrary but fine.

Process_starts: Need avoid `hash`? no.
```
def process_starts(n, starts, deadline, max_start, optim_specs, seed):
    best_h=None; best_c=np.inf
    entries=[]
    for i,st in enumerate(starts):
       if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
       try:
           h=project_sequence(st,n)
           c=float(exact_c5(h))
           h2=symmetrize(h)
           c2=float(exact_c5(h2))
           if c2 < c:
              h=h2; c=c2
           if c < best_c:
              best_c=c; best_h=h
           entries.append((c, i, h))
       except Exception:
           pass
    if not entries:
       return best_h,best_c
    entries.sort()
    # selected
    sel_count=min(max_start,len(entries))
    for r in range(sel_count):
       if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
       c,h = entries[r][0], entries[r][2]
       for spec in optim_specs:
          if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
          kind=spec[0]; beta=float(spec[1])
          if kind == 'slsqp':
              maxiter=choose_maxiter_slsqp(n, deadline-time.time())
              h2,c2=optimize_slsqp(h,n,beta,maxiter,deadline-10)
          elif kind == 'lbgfs':
              mu=float(spec[2]) if len(spec)>2 else 20.0
              maxiter=choose_maxiter_lbgfs(n, deadline-time.time())
              h2,c2=optimize_lbgfs(h,n,beta,mu,maxiter,deadline-10)
          else: continue
          if c2 < c:
              h=c2? careful: h2,c2
              c=c2; h=h2
       # random perturb maybe small
       if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
       rng=np.random.default_rng(seed + r*104729 + n)
       noise=np.zeros(n)
       # scale maybe 0.015 * min(1,n/45)?
       scale=0.02
       noise=rng.normal(0.0, scale, size=n)
       h_noisy=project_sequence(h+noise,n)
       c_noisy=float(exact_c5(h_noisy))
       if c_noisy < c:
          h=h_noisy; c=c_noisy
       # one lbgfs on noisy if specs not?
       if time.time() > deadline - 25: break
       maxiter=choose_maxiter_lbgfs(n, deadline-time.time())
       h2,c2=optimize_lbgfs(h,n,30.0,20.0,maxiter,deadline-10)
       if c2 < c: h,c=h2,c2
       # sym and evaluate
       hs=symmetrize(h); cs=exact...
       if cs<c: h,c=hs,cs
       if c < best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h
    return best_h,best_c
```

Fine adjust:
```
for _ in range(8):
   res = target - float(y.sum())
   if abs(res) < 1e-12: break
   mask = (y > 1e-9) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-9)
   if np.any(mask):
       y[mask] += res / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
   else:
       # if res>0 add to max room, else subtract from min value
       if res > 0:
          idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - y))
          y[idx] = min(1.0, y[idx] + res)
       else:
          idx = int(np.argmax(y))
          y[idx] = max(0.0, y[idx] + res)
   y = np.clip(y,0.0,1.0)
```
If distribute over mask causes some to exceed bounds, next iterations fix. Good.

Now, let's review mathematical indexing once more to avoid gradient sign error. Suppose n=2, h=[a,b], target=1. Values:
C0=dx(a(1-a)+b(1-b))
Cplus[0]=dx(a(1-b))
Cminus[0]=dx(b(1-a))
Smooth H weights w0,wp,wm. Gradient formula:
grad0 = w0*dx*(1-2a) + wp*dx*(1-b) - wm*dx*b
grad1 = w0*dx*(1-2b) + wm*dx*(1-a) - wp*dx*a
Direct derivative:
Cplus = dx(a - ab): grad0 = dx(1-b), grad1=dx(-a). Good.
Cminus=dx(b - ab): grad0=dx(-b), grad1=dx(1-a). Good. Matches. Good.

Now, exact C with correlate: for n=2, np.correlate(h,1-h,'full') outputs maybe [a(1-b), a(1-a)+b(1-b), b(1-a)]. Max same. Good.

Potential problem: In smooth_calc, `Cminus` values for s correspond to shift -s, but in `np.correlate` order maybe plus/minus reversed. Set same. Good.

Now, if we symmetrize, h_i=h_{n-1-i}. Cplus and Cminus values related; symmetric gradient should be symmetric. Good.

Let's consider adding a "strict exact objective" in H? H values from our formulas should match exact. But due floating and use `np.correlate` in exact, discrepancy maybe 1e-15. okay.

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.time()
    hard = t_start + float(budget_s)
    deadline = hard - 4.0
    seed_int = int(seed)
    init = globals().get('initial_h_values', None)
    best_h = None; best_c = float('inf')
    if init is not None:
       try:
          p = np.asarray(init, dtype=float).ravel()
          if p.size > 0:
             p = project_sequence(p, p.size)
             p2 = symmetrize(p)
             c = float(exact_c5(p)); c2=float(exact_c5(p2))
             if c2 < c: p=p2; c=c2
             best_h=p; best_c=c
       except Exception: pass
    if best_h is None:
       fallbacks = [make_sine_start(45,1.0,0.0), make_fourier_start(45,seed_int+1,6,7), make_blur_start(45,seed_int+2,0.2)]
       for st in fallbacks:
          try:
             h=symmetrize(project_sequence(st,45)); c=float(exact_c5(h))
             if c<best_c: best_h=h; best_c=c
          except Exception: pass
    if best_h is None:
       best_h=make_sine_start(45,1.0,0.0); best_c=float(exact_c5(best_h))
    n0 = len(best_h)
    ns=[]
    for mult in [1,2,4,8]:
       n = int(round(n0*mult))
       if n < 30: n=30
       if n > 540: n=540
       ns.append(n)
    for n in [45,90,180,360,540]: ns.append(n)
    ns = sorted(set(ns))
    if len(ns)>5: ns=ns[:5]
    if len(ns)==1: fracs=[1.0]
    elif len(ns)==2: fracs=[0.4,1.0]
    elif len(ns)==3: fracs=[0.25,0.65,1.0]
    elif len(ns)==4: fracs=[0.2,0.5,0.78,1.0]
    else: fracs=[0.15,0.4,0.65,0.85,1.0]
    for i,n in enumerate(ns):
       phase_deadline = min(deadline, t_start + float(budget_s)*fracs[i])
       if time.time() > phase_deadline - 12.0: continue
       # if too many candidates and small phase, skip generation? 
       if n <= 60: specs=[('slsqp',12.0),('slsqp',25.0)]; maxs=2
       elif n <= 120: specs=[('slsqp',12.0),('slsqp',25.0),('slsqp',40.0)]; maxs=3
       elif n <= 260: specs=[('slsqp',25.0),('slsqp',40.0)]; maxs=2
       else: specs=[]; maxs=1
       starts = make_candidates(n, seed_int + i*101, best_h)
       sub_h, sub_c = process_starts(n, starts, phase_deadline, maxs, specs, seed_int + 5000 + i*7)
       if sub_h is not None:
          s2 = symmetrize(sub_h); c2=float(exact_c5(s2)); csub=float(exact_c5(sub_h))
          if c2 < csub: sub_h=sub_h? Wait if c2<csub, sub_h=s2 sub_c=c2.
          if sub_c < best_c: best_c=sub_c; best_h=sub_h
    # final polish
    if time.time() < deadline - 25.0 and best_h is not None:
       n=len(best_h)
       rem = deadline - time.time()
       if n <= 120:
          h2,c2=optimize_slsqp(best_h,n,40.0,choose_maxiter_slsqp(n,rem),deadline-10)
          if c2 < best_c: best_h=h2; best_c=c2
       if time.time() < deadline - 20.0:
          h2,c2=optimize_lbgfs(best_h,n,40.0,20.0,choose_maxiter_lbgfs(n,deadline-time.time()),deadline-10)
          if c2 < best_c: best_h=h2; best_c=c2
       h2=symmetrize(best_h); c2=float(exact_c5(h2)); c1=float(exact_c5(best_h)
       if c2 < c1: best_h=h2; best_c=c2
    # final ensure project and evaluate
    best_h = project_sequence(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = float(exact_c5(best_h))
    h_sym = symmetrize(best_h)
    c_sym = float(exact_c5(h_sym))
    if c_sym < best_c:
       best_h = h_sym; best_c = c_sym
    return (list(best_h), float(best_c), int(len(best_h)))
```

```
       rng? Use one rng.
```

```
def final_random_polish(h,n,deadline,seed):
    best = project_sequence(h,n)
    best_c = float(exact_c5(best))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    xgrid = 2.0*np.arange(n)/n
    max_iter = 400 if n <= 90 else 200 if n <= 240 else 80
    scales = (0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002)
    no_improve = 0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() > deadline - 8: break
        d = np.zeros(n)
        for k in range(1,4):
            if rng.random()<0.8:
               amp = 1.0/float(k)
               if rng.random()<0.5: amp=-amp
               d += amp*np.cos(np.pi*float(k)*xgrid + 2*np.pi*rng.random())
        d = d - np.mean(d)
        normd = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if normd < 1e-12:
            d = rng.normal(0,1,n); d=d-np.mean(d); normd=...
        d /= max(normd,1e-12)
        improved=False
        for sc in scales:
            cand = project_sequence(best + sc*d, n)
            c = float(exact_c5(cand))
            if c < best_c - 1e-8:
                best=cand; best_c=c; improved=True; break
        if improved: no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve > 40: break
            # slightly shrink scales? not necessary
    h2 = symmetrize(best); c2=...
    if c2<best_c: best=h2; best_c=c2
    return best,best_c
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


class TimeLimitError(Exception):
    pass


_current_deadline = 0.0
_current_beta = 10.0
_pen_mu = 20.0
_constraint_target = 0.0


def project_sequence(x, n=None):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    if n is None:
        n = x.size
    else:
        n = int(n)
    if x.size != n:
        y = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
        m = min(n, x.size)
        if m > 0:
            y[:m] = x[:m]
        x = y
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    if n == 0:
        return x
    lo = -(float(np.max(x)) + 1e-12)
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(x))
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(10):
        res = target - float(np.sum(y))
        if abs(res) < 1e-13:
            break
        mask = (y > 1e-9) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-9)
        if np.count_nonzero(mask) > 0:
            y[mask] += res / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        else:
            if res > 0:
                idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - y))
                y[idx] = min(1.0, y[idx] + res)
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmax(y))
                y[idx] = max(0.0, y[idx] + res)
        y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return y


def exact_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def symmetrize(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return h
    s = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    return project_sequence(s, n)


def smooth_calc(x, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = x.size
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    s0 = float(np.sum(x)) - float(np.dot(x, x))
    C0 = dx * s0
    if n <= 1:
        C = np.array([C0], dtype=float)
        w = np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
        grad = np.array([w[0] * dx * (1.0 - 2.0 * x[0])], dtype=float)
        H = C0
        return H, grad, C0
    prefix = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    prefix[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(x, out=prefix[1:])
    total = prefix[n]
    prod = np.correlate(x, x, mode="valid")
    idx = np.arange(1, n, dtype=int)
    Cplus = dx * (prefix[n - idx] - prod)
    Cminus = dx * (total - prefix[idx] - prod)
    C = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    C[0] = C0
    C[1:n] = Cplus
    C[n:2 * n - 1] = Cminus
    cmax = float(np.max(C))
    if beta <= 1e-8:
        w = np.full_like(C, 1.0 / float(C.size))
        H = cmax
    else:
        z = beta * (C - cmax)
        z = np.where(z < 0.0, z, 0.0)
        expz = np.exp(np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0))
        ss = float(np.sum(expz))
        if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
            w = np.zeros_like(C)
            w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
            H = cmax
        else:
            w = expz / ss
            H = cmax + float(np.log(ss)) / float(beta)
    grad = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    w0 = w[0] * dx
    grad[:] = w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
    wp = w[1:n] * dx
    wm = w[n:2 * n - 1] * dx
    for s in range(1, n):
        a = float(wp[s - 1])
        b = float(wm[s - 1])
        if a <= 1e-12 and b <= 1e-12:
            continue
        ls = n - s
        if a > 0.0:
            grad[:ls] += a * (1.0 - x[s:])
            grad[s:] -= a * x[:ls]
        if b > 0.0:
            grad[:ls] -= b * x[s:]
            grad[s:] += b * (1.0 - x[:ls])
    return float(H), grad, cmax


def _eq_fun(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - _constraint_target)


def _eq_jac(x):
    return np.ones_like(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), dtype=float)


def _obj_eq(x):
    if time.time() > _current_deadline:
        raise TimeLimitError()
    H, _, _ = smooth_calc(x, _current_beta)
    return float(H)


def _grad_eq(x):
    if time.time() > _current_deadline:
        raise TimeLimitError()
    _, g, _ = smooth_calc(x, _current_beta)
    return g


def _obj_pen(x):
    if time.time() > _current_deadline:
        raise TimeLimitError()
    H, g, _ = smooth_calc(x, _current_beta)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    target = 0.5 * float(x.size)
    dev = s - target
    return float(H + _pen_mu * dev * dev)


def _grad_pen(x):
    if time.time() > _current_deadline:
        raise TimeLimitError()
    H, g, _ = smooth_calc(x, _current_beta)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    target = 0.5 * float(x.size)
    dev = s - target
    return g + (2.0 * _pen_mu * dev) * np.ones_like(x, dtype=float)


def optimize_slsqp(h, n, beta, maxiter, deadline):
    global _current_deadline, _current_beta, _constraint_target
    n = int(n)
    x0 = project_sequence(h, n)
    if maxiter <= 0:
        return x0, float(exact_c5(x0))
    _current_deadline = max(float(deadline), time.time() + 0.1)
    _current_beta = float(beta)
    _constraint_target = 0.5 * float(n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    constraints = [{"type": "eq", "fun": _eq_fun, "jac": _eq_jac}]
    options = {"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-8, "disp": 0}
    xbest = x0
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _obj_eq,
            x0,
            jac=_grad_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=constraints,
            method="SLSQP",
            options=options,
        )
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            xbest = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
    except TimeLimitError:
        xbest = x0
    except Exception:
        xbest = x0
    xbest = project_sequence(xbest, n)
    c1 = float(exact_c5(xbest))
    x2 = symmetrize(xbest)
    c2 = float(exact_c5(x2))
    if c2 < c1:
        xbest = x2
        c1 = c2
    return xbest, c1


def optimize_lbgfs(h, n, beta, mu, maxiter, deadline):
    global _current_deadline, _current_beta, _pen_mu
    n = int(n)
    x0 = project_sequence(h, n)
    if maxiter <= 0:
        return x0, float(exact_c5(x0))
    _current_deadline = max(float(deadline), time.time() + 0.1)
    _current_beta = float(beta)
    _pen_mu = float(mu)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    options = {"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-8, "maxls": 20, "disp": 0}
    xbest = x0
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _obj_pen,
            x0,
            jac=_grad_pen,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options=options,
        )
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            xbest = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
    except TimeLimitError:
        xbest = x0
    except Exception:
        xbest = x0
    xbest = project_sequence(xbest, n)
    c1 = float(exact_c5(xbest))
    x2 = symmetrize(xbest)
    c2 = float(exact_c5(x2))
    if c2 < c1:
        xbest = x2
        c1 = c2
    return xbest, c1


def choose_maxiter_slsqp(n, remaining):
    if remaining < 60.0:
        return 30
    if n <= 60:
        return 260
    if n <= 120:
        return 220
    if n <= 240:
        return 130
    return 70


def choose_maxiter_lbgfs(n, remaining):
    if remaining < 45.0:
        return 30
    if n <= 120:
        return 260
    if n <= 250:
        return 170
    return 90


def gaussian_kernel(radius, sigma):
    t = np.arange(-radius, radius + 1, dtype=float)
    k = np.exp(-0.5 * (t / float(sigma)) ** 2)
    s = float(np.sum(k))
    if s <= 0.0:
        k = np.ones(radius * 2 + 1, dtype=float)
    else:
        k = k / s
    return k


def make_sine_start(n, amp, phase):
    n = int(n)
    x = 2.0 * np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * float(amp) * np.cos(np.pi * x - float(phase))
    return project_sequence(h, n)


def make_fourier_start(n, seed, terms=6, max_freq=7):
    n = int(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = 2.0 * np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    max_freq = int(max_freq)
    terms = int(terms)
    if max_freq > 0:
        freqs = rng.choice(np.arange(1, max_freq + 1, dtype=int), size=min(terms, max_freq), replace=False)
    else:
        freqs = np.array([], dtype=int)
    for k in freqs:
        amp = 1.0 / float(k ** 0.7)
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            amp = -amp
        ph = 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.random())
        g += amp * np.cos(np.pi * float(k) * x + ph)
    m = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
    if m > 1e-15:
        g = g / m
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * g
    return project_sequence(h, n)


def make_blur_start(n, seed, sigma_frac=0.15):
    n = int(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    y = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    half = n // 2
    if half > 0:
        y[:] = 0.0
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=half, replace=False)
        y[idx] = 1.0
    if n % 2 == 1:
        j = int(rng.integers(n))
        y[j] = 0.5
    sigma = max(2, int(round(float(n) * float(sigma_frac))))
    radius = int(min(4 * sigma, n))
    kernel = gaussian_kernel(radius, sigma)
    y = np.convolve(y, kernel, mode="same")
    return project_sequence(y, n)


def make_parent_upsample(parent, n, mode):
    n = int(n)
    if parent is None:
        return make_sine_start(n, 1.0, 0.0)
    p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = p.size
    if m == 0:
        return make_sine_start(n, 1.0, 0.0)
    if n == m:
        return project_sequence(p, n)
    if mode == "repeat":
        idx = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64) * m) // n
        y = p[idx]
    else:
        x_old = 2.0 * np.arange(m, dtype=float) / float(m)
        x_new = 2.0 * np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
        y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return project_sequence(y, n)


def make_candidates(n, seed, starter):
    n = int(n)
    starts = []
    if starter is not None:
        starts.append(make_parent_upsample(starter, n, "repeat"))
        starts.append(make_parent_upsample(starter, n, "interp"))
        starts.append(symmetrize(make_parent_upsample(starter, n, "interp")))
    phases = [
        (1.0, 0.0),
        (1.0, 0.5),
        (1.0, -0.5),
        (0.9, 0.0),
        (0.8, 0.7),
    ]
    for a, ph in phases:
        starts.append(make_sine_start(n, a, ph))
    if n <= 90:
        fourier_count = 6
        blur_counts = [(0.08, 3), (0.15, 3), (0.30, 2)]
    elif n <= 200:
        fourier_count = 4
        blur_counts = [(0.10, 2), (0.20, 2), (0.35, 1)]
    else:
        fourier_count = 2
        blur_counts = [(0.12, 1), (0.25, 1)]
    for i in range(fourier_count):
        starts.append(make_fourier_start(n, int(seed) + 1000 + n + i, 6, 7))
    for frac, cnt in blur_counts:
        for j in range(cnt):
            starts.append(make_blur_start(n, int(seed) + 2000 + n + j + int(frac * 100), frac))
    return starts


def process_starts(n, starts, deadline, max_start, optim_specs, seed):
    n = int(n)
    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    entries = []
    for i, st in enumerate(starts):
        if time.time() > float(deadline) - 30.0:
            break
        try:
            h = project_sequence(st, n)
            c = float(exact_c5(h))
            h2 = symmetrize(h)
            c2 = float(exact_c5(h2))
            if c2 < c:
                h = h2
                c = c2
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = h
            entries.append((c, i, h))
        except Exception:
            pass
    if not entries:
        return best_h, best_c
    entries.sort()
    sel = min(int(max_start), len(entries))
    for r in range(sel):
        if time.time() > float(deadline) - 20.0:
            break
        c, _, h = entries[r]
        h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
        for spec in optim_specs:
            if time.time() > float(deadline) - 20.0:
                break
            kind = spec[0]
            beta = float(spec[1])
            if kind == "slsqp":
                it = choose_maxiter_slsqp(n, float(deadline) - time.time())
                h2, c2 = optimize_slsqp(h, n, beta, it, float(deadline) - 10.0)
            elif kind == "lbgfs":
                mu = float(spec[2]) if len(spec) > 2 else 20.0
                it = choose_maxiter_lbgfs(n, float(deadline) - time.time())
                h2, c2 = optimize_lbgfs(h, n, beta, mu, it, float(deadline) - 10.0)
            else:
                continue
            if c2 < c:
                h = h2
                c = c2
        if time.time() > float(deadline) - 35.0:
            break
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 104729 * (r + 1) + n)
        noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=n)
        h_noisy = project_sequence(h + noise, n)
        c_noisy = float(exact_c5(h_noisy))
        if c_noisy < c:
            h = h_noisy
            c = c_noisy
        if time.time() > float(deadline) - 25.0:
            break
        it2 = choose_maxiter_lbgfs(n, float(deadline) - time.time())
        h2, c2 = optimize_lbgfs(h, n, 30.0, 20.0, it2, float(deadline) - 10.0)
        if c2 < c:
            h = h2
            c = c2
        h3 = symmetrize(h)
        c3 = float(exact_c5(h3))
        if c3 < c:
            h = h3
            c = c3
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h
    return best_h, best_c


def final_random_polish(h, n, deadline, seed):
    n = int(n)
    best = project_sequence(h, n)
    best_c = float(exact_c5(best))
    if n == 0:
        return best, best_c
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    xgrid = 2.0 * np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
    if n <= 90:
        max_iter = 350
    elif n <= 240:
        max_iter = 160
    else:
        max_iter = 70
    scales = (0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002)
    no_improve = 0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() > float(deadline) - 8.0:
            break
        d = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for k in range(1, 4):
            if rng.random() < 0.85:
                amp = 1.0 / float(k)
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    amp = -amp
                d += amp * np.cos(np.pi * float(k) * xgrid + 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.random()))
        d = d - np.mean(d)
        nd = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if nd < 1e-12:
            d = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
            d = d - np.mean(d)
            nd = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if nd > 1e-12:
            d = d / nd
        improved = False
        for sc in scales:
            cand = project_sequence(best + float(sc) * d, n)
            c = float(exact_c5(cand))
            if c < best_c - 1e-9:
                best = cand
                best_c = c
                improved = True
                break
        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 40:
                break
    h2 = symmetrize(best)
    c2 = float(exact_c5(h2))
    if c2 < best_c:
        best = h2
        best_c = c2
    return best, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.time()
    hard_stop = t_start + max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = hard_stop - 4.0
    seed_int = int(seed)
    init = globals().get("initial_h_values", None)
    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    if init is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(init, dtype=float).ravel()
            if p.size > 0:
                p = project_sequence(p, p.size)
                p2 = symmetrize(p)
                c = float(exact_c5(p))
                c2 = float(exact_c5(p2))
                if c2 < c:
                    p = p2
                    c = c2
                best_h = p
                best_c = c
        except Exception:
            pass
    if best_h is None:
        fallbacks = [
            make_sine_start(45, 1.0, 0.0),
            make_fourier_start(45, seed_int + 1, 6, 7),
            make_blur_start(45, seed_int + 2, 0.20),
        ]
        for st in fallbacks:
            try:
                h = symmetrize(project_sequence(st, 45))
                c = float(exact_c5(h))
                if c < best_c:
                    best_h = h
                    best_c = c
            except Exception:
                pass
    if best_h is None:
        best_h = make_sine_start(45, 1.0, 0.0)
        best_c = float(exact_c5(best_h))
    n0 = int(len(best_h))
    ns = []
    for mult in (1, 2, 4, 8):
        nn = int(round(n0 * mult))
        if nn < 30:
            nn = 30
        if nn > 540:
            nn = 540
        ns.append(nn)
    for nn in (45, 90, 180, 360, 540):
        ns.append(nn)
    ns = sorted(set(ns))
    if len(ns) > 5:
        ns = ns[:5]
    if len(ns) == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif len(ns) == 2:
        fracs = [0.40, 1.00]
    elif len(ns) == 3:
        fracs = [0.25, 0.65, 1.00]
    elif len(ns) == 4:
        fracs = [0.20, 0.50, 0.78, 1.00]
    else:
        fracs = [0.15, 0.40, 0.65, 0.85, 1.00]
    for i, nn in enumerate(ns):
        phase_deadline = min(deadline, t_start + float(budget_s) * fracs[i])
        if time.time() > phase_deadline - 12.0:
            continue
        if nn <= 60:
            specs = [("slsqp", 12.0), ("slsqp", 25.0)]
            max_start = 2
        elif nn <= 120:
            specs = [("slsqp", 12.0), ("slsqp", 25.0), ("slsqp", 40.0)]
            max_start = 3
        elif nn <= 260:
            specs = [("slsqp", 25.0), ("slsqp", 40.0)]
            max_start = 2
        else:
            specs = []
            max_start = 1
        starts = make_candidates(nn, seed_int + 101 * i, best_h)
        sh, sc = process_starts(nn, starts, phase_deadline, max_start, specs, seed_int + 5000 + 7 * i)
        if sh is not None:
            c_sub = float(exact_c5(sh))
            sh2 = symmetrize(sh)
            c_sub2 = float(exact_c5(sh2))
            if c_sub2 < c_sub:
                sh = sh2
                c_sub = c_sub2
            if c_sub < best_c:
                best_h = sh
                best_c = c_sub
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
        nn = int(len(best_h))
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if nn <= 120:
            h2, c2 = optimize_slsqp(best_h, nn, 40.0, choose_maxiter_slsqp(nn, rem), deadline - 10.0)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h = h2
                best_c = c2
        if time.time() < deadline - 22.0:
            h2, c2 = optimize_lbgfs(best_h, nn, 40.0, 20.0, choose_maxiter_lbgfs(nn, deadline - time.time()), deadline - 10.0)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h = h2
                best_c = c2
        if time.time() < deadline - 18.0:
            h2, c2 = final_random_polish(best_h, nn, deadline - 5.0, seed_int + 999983)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h = h2
                best_c = c2
    if best_h is None:
        best_h = make_sine_start(45, 1.0, 0.0)
    best_h = project_sequence(best_h, int(len(best_h)))
    best_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float).copy()
    best_c = float(exact_c5(best_h))
    hs = symmetrize(best_h)
    cs = float(exact_c5(hs))
    if cs < best_c:
        best_h = hs
        best_c = cs
    return (list(best_h), float(best_c), int(len(best_h)))
```
